<span class="input-group-btn">
<button class="search__button" id="search-submit" type="button">

<script>
var request = null;
$(document).ready(function(){
	$('#advanced_search').off();
	$(document).bind('keyup', function(e) {
		if(e.keyCode == 13){
			// $('#search-submit').trigger('click');
		}
	});
	
	$('#advanced_search').on('input',function(){
		if($(this).val().length >= 3){
			seachRequest(true);
		}else{
			$('.search-wrap').html('');
			$('.search-wrap').fadeOut(100);
		}
	});

	if($('#advanced_search').val()){
		seachRequest(false);
	}


	$('#advanced_search').on('focus',function(){
		if($('.search-cat-item').length){
			$('.search-wrap').fadeIn(100);
		}
	});

	$(document).on('click',function(e){
		if(!$(e.target).is('.search-wrap') && !$(e.target).parents('.search-wrap').length && !$(e.target).is('#advanced_search')){
			$('.search-wrap').fadeOut(100);
		}
	});
	$(document).keyup(function(e) {
		if (e.keyCode == 27) {
			if($('#advanced_search').is(":focus")){
				$('.search-wrap').fadeOut(100);
				$('#advanced_search').blur();
			}
		}
	});
});
function seachRequest(show){

	if( request != null ) {
		request.abort();
		request = null;
	}

	request =  $.ajax({
		url: 'https://lodka5.com.ua/index.php?route=extension/module/advanced_search/searchRequest',
		dataType: 'json',
		type: 'post',
		data: $('#search input'),
		beforeRequest: function(){

		},
		success: function(json){
			$('.search-wrap').html('');
			if(!$.isEmptyObject(json)) {
				
				if(json['categories'].length > 0) {
					$('.search-wrap').prepend('<div class="search-cat-item" id="search-categories"><p class="advanced-search__title">Категории</p</div>');
					$.each(json['categories'],function(index,e){
						$('#search-categories').append('<div class="advanced-search__elements"  data-target-href="' + e['href'] + '"><a class="advanced-search__link" href="' + e['href'] + '">' + e['name'] + '</a></div>');
					});
				}

				
				if(json['products'].length > 0) {
					$('.search-wrap').append('<div class="search-cat-item" id="search-products"></div>');
					$.each(json['products'],function(index,e){
						$('#search-products').append('<div class="advanced-search__item" data-target-href="' + e['href'] + '"><a class="advanced-search__image-wrapper" href="' + e['href'] + '"><img class="advanced-search__image" src="' + e['image'] + '" alt="' + e['name'] + '" /></a><div class="advanced-search__caption"><a class="advanced-search__link-title" href="' + e['href'] + '">' + e['name'] + '</a><div class="advanced-search__price">' + e['price'] + '</div></div></div>');
					});
				}
				
				if(json['keywords'].length > 0){
					$('.search-wrap').append('<div class="search-cat-item" id="search-keywords"><p class="advanced-search__title">Похожие запросы</p></div>');
					$.each(json['keywords'],function(index,e){
						$('#search-keywords').append('<div class="advanced-search__elements"  data-target-href="' + e['href'] + '"><a class="advanced-search__link" href="' + e['href'] + '">' + e['keyword'] + '</a></div>');
					});
				}

				if (json['categories'].length == 0 && json['products'].length == 0 && json['keywords'].length == 0) {
					$('.search-wrap').append('<div class="search-cat-item">' + json['no_products'] + '</div>');
				}

			} else {
				$('.search-wrap').html().fadeOut(100);
			}
		},
		error: function(xhr,asdasd,thrownExeption){
			console.log(xhr.responseText);
		},
		complete: function(json){
			if($.isEmptyObject(json)){
				$('.search-wrap').html('').fadeOut(100);
			}
			if(show && $('.search-cat-item').length){
				$('.search-wrap').fadeIn(100);
			}
		}
	});
}
</script>
